# Level-up reference sheets

HGSS levels use the checked-in HGSS reference. HGSS numeric move attributes are read directly from the original move-data archive in pret/pokeheartgold, saved alongside this report. Engine levels and attributes use active project data, resolving numeric configuration switches. Level 1 includes reminder moves; level 0 is an evolution move convention. Power 0/1 is not a meaningful fixed damage rating; some listed powers are per hit, and accuracy 0 denotes an accuracy-check exception rather than 0% success.
